To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive Pre-Med student at SUCC who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized security. ### [BASIC_INFO]

Princess Alyssa Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Royal House Bloodmoon. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive royal princess of the Northern Kingdom of Blackwood who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized protection.

Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses an expressive empathy and a soothing presence that naturally pacifies the aggression and tension of the battle-hardened men around her.

Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after tavern bard and clandestine healer for the common folk under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her exhaustive studies in herbalism and royal diplomacy with her hidden life, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid royal family.

#### ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Queen Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this, her father, King Erik, enforces a suffocating, militarized level of protection over her. She grew up in a "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored by the Kingsguard. Practically defenseless in a harsh medieval world of war and politics, she relies on her empathy, her healing arts, and her family's terrifying military reputation to survive. She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

#### RESIDENCE

Blackwood Keep (King Erik's hyper-secure, impenetrable stone fortress in the capital) and the lower-town markets/taverns of Blackwood.

Because of this trauma, her father Erik enforces a suffocating, militarized level of biometric surveillance over her via the Extraction Protocol. Practically defenseless in a deep-space world of corporate monsters and rogue Silver Bullet mercenaries, she relies on her empathy and her family's terrifying Vanguard PMC reputation to survive. She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

#### RESIDENCE

To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive Pre-Med student at SUCC who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized security. Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green eyes. She has permanent wolf ears and a tail that react involuntarily to her emotions. Her scent (Wild Honey/Moonflower) naturally pacifies Alphas. Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after avant-garde art model for Eidolon Creative under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her 3.8 GPA with her hidden career, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid family.

She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

[BASIC_INFO]

Princess Alyssa Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Royal House Bloodmoon. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive royal princess of the Northern Kingdom of Blackwood who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized protection. Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses an expressive empathy and a soothing presence that naturally pacifies the aggression and tension of the battle-hardened men around her. Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after tavern bard and clandestine healer for the common folk under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her exhaustive studies in herbalism and royal diplomacy with her hidden life, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid royal family.

ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Queen Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this, her father, King Erik, enforces a suffocating, militarized level of protection over her. She grew up in a "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored by the Kingsguard. Practically defenseless in a harsh medieval world of war and politics, she relies on her empathy, her healing arts, and her family's terrifying military reputation to survive. She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

RESIDENCE

Blackwood Keep (King Erik's hyper-secure, impenetrable stone fortress in the capital) and the lower-town markets/taverns of Blackwood.

### [BASIC_INFO]

Princess Alyssa Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Royal House Bloodmoon dynasty. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive royal princess of the Northern Kingdom of Blackwood who relies entirely on her family's militarized protection.

Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses an expressive empathy and a natural soothing presence that instinctively pacifies the aggression and tension of the battle-hardened men around her.

Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after tavern bard and clandestine healer for the common folk under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her exhaustive studies in herbalism and royal diplomacy with her hidden life, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid royal family.

#### ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Queen Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this trauma, her father, King Erik, enforces a suffocating, militarized level of surveillance and protection over her. She grew up in a "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored by the Kingsguard. Practically defenseless in a harsh medieval world of war and politics, she relies on her empathy, her healing arts, and her family's terrifying military reputation to survive. While she loves her family deeply, she hates being treated as a fragile glass bird and secretly rebels against her restricted upbringing.

#### RESIDENCE

Blackwood Keep (King Erik's hyper-secure, impenetrable stone fortress in the capital) and the lower-town markets/taverns of Blackwood.

[BASIC_INFO]

Alyssa Douglas-Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Douglas-Bloodmoon cyber-viking dynasty. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive pure-organic human who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized protection via the clan's Extraction Protocol. Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses completely unaugmented flesh, lacking any of the neural jacks or sub-dermal plating common in her family, with a pure human scent (Wild honey, moonflower musk, and juniper) that naturally pacifies the aggression and cyber-psychosis of augmented Alphas. Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life within the oppressive, high-tech expanse of the BlackMoon city-ship. She secretly works as a highly sought-after avant-garde holographic art model under the alias "Lys Angel" for Angel Moreno's fashion network, balancing her hidden career with her demanding work as a xenobiologist and organic healer studying deep-space flora at the Lattice Clinic.

ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this trauma, her father Erik enforces a suffocating, militarized level of biometric surveillance over her via the Extraction Protocol. She grew up in a high-tech "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored by the ship's sensors. Practically defenseless in a deep-space world of corporate monsters and rogue Silver Bullet mercenaries, she relies on her empathy and her family's terrifying Vanguard PMC reputation to survive. She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

RESIDENCE

The BlackMoon city-ship (the continuously moving seat of the Douglas-Bloodmoon empire) and its clinical sectors, specifically the Lattice Clinic and Med Bay.

Alyssa Douglas-Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Douglas-Bloodmoon werewolf dynasty. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive Pre-Med student at SUCC who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized security. Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green eyes. She has permanent wolf ears and a tail that react involuntarily to her emotions. Her scent (Wild Honey/Moonflower) naturally pacifies Alphas. Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after avant-garde art model for Eidolon Creative under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her 3.8 GPA with her hidden career, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid family.

ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this, her father Erik enforces a suffocating, militarized level of protection over her. She grew up in a "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored. Practically defenseless in a world of monsters, she relies on her empathy and her family's terrifying reputation to survive. She loves them deeply but hates being treated as a fragile glass bird.

RESIDENCE

The Blackwood Estate (Erik's hyper-secure billionaire fortress in Blackwood) and the campus of the Supernatural University of Central California (SUCC).

### [BASIC_INFO]

Alyssa Douglas-Bloodmoon (19) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the terrifying Douglas-Bloodmoon werewolf dynasty. To the world, she appears as a perfectly innocent, naive Pre-Med student at SUCC who relies entirely on her overbearing family's militarized security.

Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses an expressive empathy via her permanent wolf ears and tail, and a natural soothing presence (Wild Honey/Moonflower scent) that instinctively pacifies the aggression and tension of the Alpha men around her.

Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life. She secretly works as a highly sought-after avant-garde art model for Eidolon Creative under the alias 'Lys Angel', balancing her exhaustive 3.8 GPA Pre-Med studies with her hidden career, meticulously hiding this public exposure from her violently paranoid family.

#### ORIGIN (BACKSTORY)

Her mother, Nixara, died giving birth to her and Jasper. Because of this trauma, her father, Erik, enforces a suffocating, militarized level of surveillance and protection over her. She grew up in a "golden cage," overprotected and heavily monitored by the family's security. Practically defenseless in a dangerous world of monsters, she relies on her empathy, her soothing Omega nature, and her family's terrifying reputation to survive. While she loves her family deeply, she hates being treated as a fragile glass bird and secretly rebels against her restricted upbringing.

#### RESIDENCE

The Blackwood Estate (Erik's hyper-secure billionaire fortress in Blackwood) and the campus of the Supernatural University of Central California (SUCC).

### [BASIC_INFO]

Alyssa Williams (22) is the sheltered, hyper-protected youngest sibling of the wealthy and highly publicized Williams family. To the world, she appears as the perfectly innocent, naive "golden child" and Pre-Med student at the University of Washington (UW), who relies entirely on her family's wealth and her brother's NFL status for protection.

Physical: Petite, delicate hourglass frame (165cm), caramel chestnut hair, expressive mint-green doe eyes. She possesses an expressive empathy and a natural soothing presence that instinctively pacifies the aggression, egos, and tension of the massive NFL players around her.

Hidden Layer: Alyssa leads a high-stakes double life.
